# NEXT ACTIVE TASK — T02: Snapshot dashboard MVP

> **PARTIAL — policy revised by user; full local suites PASS; focused native V01 READY.**
> User: “Lets start the
> implementation.” (2026-11-05), following the compact single-line mockup and
> accessory-first/grouped-Tool refinements. Approval covers the 14-file manifest,
> serial queue and local checks below, not installations, gameplay actions or release.
> R5 presentation polish explicitly approved by user “Yes proceed” (2026-11-06).

## Goal

Build a dependency-free `CraftArmy` addon showing saved crafting-alt/profession
snapshots in a native dashboard. Deliver the required columns, three independent
equipment indicators, tool/enchant stat categories, and exact knowledge accounting
where supported. Start client validation with Inscription; support discovery of the
eight D05 professions without claiming untested mappings or complete first-release
coverage. T02 is a dashboard MVP, not dashboard acceptance for all professions.

## Why (pointer)

- [D05 — scope/delivery](../../../PLAN.md#d05--initial-scope-audience-and-delivery-order-),

- [Q03 — field/API evidence](../../questions/Q03.md),
  [Q06 — implementation preparation](../../questions/Q06.md),
  [T01 evidence](../../diagnostics/evidence.md). Detailed design remains a proposal,
  not authorization to add guide/inventory/integration work.

## Design — numbered pieces

- [x] **1. Pure snapshot core and schema.** _Delivered; execution pending shared fixtures._ Implement `Core.lua` and document schema
  in `docs/dashboard/schema.md`. No WoW calls at pure-module load time. Use namespace
  injection and one production SavedVariables name, `CraftArmyDB`, schema 1, isolated
  from diagnostic data; do not import/rewrite diagnostic samples.
  - Key characters by GUID; store name/realm as labels. Key professions by expansion
    skill-line ID with separate parent ID, profession enum, expansion identity and
    localized labels. Never key records by translated names, list position or gear ID.
  - Each field stores latest attempt `{status, attemptedAt, source, reason}` plus
    last successful `{status, value, observedAt, build, generation}` if present.
    Statuses: `observed`, `empty`, `unknown`, `unsupported`, `error`,
    `not_applicable`. Preserve actual zero/false. A transient failure preserves known
    values with stale/last-observed presentation; it never confirms empty/zero/no enchant.
  - Treat profession identity, each equipped instance, and each tree accounting
    result as coherent units. Do not combine ranks/caps from different scans, or
    enchant/stats from a replaced item with the new item's rarity/link. Slot existence
    can be known while item metadata is loading. On changed/removed gear invalidate
    dependent metadata; retain old-item history only as history, not the new item.
  - Whitelist finite primitives/tables and bound traversal; reject cycles, invalid
    IDs/times, userdata/functions and malformed observations. Initialize only absent
    DBs. Unsupported future schema or invalid existing DB disables writes with an
    explanation, not a reset. No silent eviction of characters/archives.
  - Support active generations and immutable archived generations with reason/time.
    Retirement/unlearning excludes records from active views/totals/warnings.
    Relearning/reactivating requires a new successful own-profession scan; do not
    restore old values as current. Deletion requires explicit scoped UI confirmation.

- [!] **2. Evidence registry and knowledge engine.** _Pure engine/initial fixtures and
  eight-family registry delivered. Production numeric eligibility is field-blocked:
  pinned display/API source does not prove all-rank unit KP costs; other Midnight
  expansion IDs remain pending (only 2913 is evidenced). Independent pieces proceed._ _(depends on #1)_ Implement
  `Data.lua` and pure `Knowledge.lua`; add fixtures with this piece.
  - Registry covers the eight supported profession families, separate from observed
    Midnight expansion IDs. Source-check IDs/expansion membership; no numeric-range,
    localized-name or skill-cap heuristics to declare a profession Midnight. Unknown
    expansion mapping is an explained pending scan, not data attributed to 2913.
  - Seed only the scoped client-evidenced Inscription Moxie 2913→3261 and enchant
    8005→Multicraft category, with provenance/build in registry/evidence. Concentration
    mapping comes from the profession API, not a universal 3165 default. Never seed
    user balances, gear, rank vectors or hypothetical knowledge totals as runtime data.
  - Exact path inputs: config/tab/path IDs and children, node `ranksPurchased`,
    `ranksIncreased`, `currentRank`, `maxRanks`; unlock entry ID from
    `C_ProfSpecs.GetUnlockEntryForPath(pathID)` and its `maxRanks` from
    `C_Traits.GetEntryInfo(configID, entryID)`; committed/staged and coverage evidence.
    Keep missing unlock metadata **unknown**, including missing entry info; do not
    copy Blizzard UI's missing-entry fallback as proof of zero unlock ranks.
  - For the ordinary verified one-KP-per-spend-rank case, with explicitly no staged
    changes, zero non-purchased increases and `currentRank == ranksPurchased`, let
    `u` be that path's observed unlock-entry maxRanks (0 or 1): purchased KP is
    `ranksPurchased > 0 and ranksPurchased - u or 0`; spendable capacity is
    `maxRanks - u`. Require nonnegative integral results and purchased ≤ capacity.
    **Never universally subtract one or sum currentRank as purchased KP.** Establish
    spend-rank/cost semantics from pinned source/API evidence; formula eligibility
    is explicit, not inferred merely because numbers look plausible.
  - Free/increased ranks, variable/unknown costs, unknown staging or unexpected entry
    structure make affected accounting unknown unless a source-verified exact rule
    is documented and covered within this scope; no proportional allocation or
    guessing. Keep raw evidence for follow-up, not fabricated purchased points.
  - Traverse root and every child path once per tree, including locked/uninvested
    branches in capacity. Deduplicate IDs; cycles/shared ownership/missing metadata
    or limits make completeness false, never a smaller complete tree. Limits: 8 tabs,
    64 paths/tree, 16 children/path. Retain coverage and reason when exceeded.
  - Sum valid unique path results across the whole top-level tree. Publish a new
    numeric tree aggregate only with complete eligible inputs from one stable scan;
    otherwise preserve an older coherent tree as last observed or show `?`.
    Unspent KP is separate and zero is valid. Shared currency 3902 spent is a
    profession-level reconciliation **once**, never added across tabs. Where its
    applicability is verified, disagreement blocks promotion of new totals, not
    retroactive editing of ranks. Absence is unknown, not zero.
  - Raw 277, shared spent 260, and proposed tree splits/caps from T01 are historical
    reconciliation hypotheses. Do not hardcode them or assert those capacities.
    Source reference: Q03's pinned `ProfessionsSpecPathMixin:GetRanks()`; displayed
    ranks alone do not prove purchased-point semantics. Live UI comparison remains
    an acceptance gate even after source-backed calculations and fixtures pass.

- [!] **3. Read-only current-character collectors.** _Guarded adapters/async collector
  delivered; knowledge unit-cost proof, other expansion mappings, authoritative
  automatic-unlearning contract, base/enchant stat attribution and client behavior
  remain field-blocked. No production numeric KP eligibility asserted._ _(depends on #1, #2)_ Implement
  `Collectors.lua` with injected API adapters and per-field protected error handling.
  - Collect build/locale/time/current-character identity and reliable learned-primary-
    profession roster at login; collect detailed Midnight data when the player opens
    their own ready profession or explicitly requests `/ca scan`. Do not open/switch
    professions, force-load other addons, or collect offline alts via live APIs.
  - Detailed scan requires positive own-player context: profession frame visible;
    linked/guild/NPC/changing flags explicitly false; supported expansion identity.
    Missing readiness APIs fail closed. Verify identity/config/context at start and
    finish; discard attribution on change. Combat defers collection/UI creation until
    safe. Closed/unsafe scans preserve snapshots and explain how to refresh.
    Knowledge additionally requires staged=false at both boundaries and no trait/
    config invalidation during collection; async work uses the same generation token.
    A changed generation discards the pending result rather than mixing observations.
  - Roster absence archives an unlearned profession only after an authoritative,
    complete player roster comparison. Nil/partial roster or closed profession UI
    does not establish unlearning. Without a proven roster contract, retain records
    and expose manual retirement; leave automatic unlearning acceptance incomplete.
  - Query skill/cap/modifier separately; concentration quantity/verified cap and
    recharge evidence; Moxie via registry; equipment indices from profession API;
    exact knowledge inputs/coverage from #2. Unsupported mappings affect that field,
    not the whole row. Keep unspent KP separate from shared currency spent.
  - Establish tool/accessory roles from pinned equipment UI/API evidence and record
    the rule, never from sorted index position or assumed universal slot 20. Until
    resolved, display independent slot evidence with role unknown and no invented
    tool-specific attribution. Known empty requires a valid role/location and a
    successful explicit false item-existence read, not a nil link/cache miss.
  - Read equipped instance link, independent rarity/crafted rank/icon metadata,
    numeric stat keys and permanent-enchant typed lines/link enchant ID. Ready valid
    link's explicit zero enchant field can support absence; missing/invalid field or
    no tooltip lines alone cannot. Conflicting link/tooltip results are unknown.
    Nonzero ID/typed positive line supports presence; unmapped ID has unknown stat.
    Enchant 8005 gives category only: no amount/quality inferred from tool rank/icon.
  - Do not count enchant stats twice or call returned combined stats base-only.
    Display verified base-tool stats separately where attributable. Otherwise show
    stat category/observed total with an explicit attribution-unknown label; enchant
    category stays separately visible without inventing a bonus to subtract/add.
  - Async item handling: source-check load request/event signatures; at most one
    request/item per scan, 16 pending requests, one completion retry per item, timeout
    5 seconds. Token includes GUID/profession generation/slot/link; late/mismatched
    callbacks cannot overwrite newer gear or another character/profession. No polling.
    Burst events coalesce to one scheduled scan; no periodic full-scan loop.
  - Verify event signatures before registering. Start from login, profession show/
    data-source change, equipment, currency and trait-change source events; callbacks
    only request guarded collection. Unknown events/API signatures remain unsupported,
    not guessed alternate APIs. Record actual chosen signatures in dashboard evidence.

- [x] **4. Pure dashboard view model.** _Delivered; fixture execution pending._ _(depends on #1, #2; independent of #3)_
  Implement `ViewModel.lua` using fixture snapshots, not live APIs.
  - One row per active character/profession. Columns: name/realm, profession,
    skill/cap (modifier separate), **Accessory #1, Accessory #2, Tool**,
    concentration/cap, Moxie, per-tree bars and unspent KP. The **Tool** column groups
    its gear indicator, abbreviated tool stat(s), and separately identified enchant
    yes/no/unknown plus category, all inline; no standalone Stats/Enchant columns.
    Keep three independent gear indicators. Display ordering does not authorize
    assuming numeric API slot roles; verified role mapping from #3 still applies.
    User's mockup correction requires a **single compact line** per pair: small gear
    icons, abbreviated stats and inline miniature knowledge bars, not tall character
    cards, portraits, or a separate knowledge strip. Full labels, exact breakdowns
    and modifier detail can use tooltips; keep a compact visible snapshot/stale label
    with age, and expose exact field timestamps on hover. Repeat character identity
    on separate profession rows instead of creating expanded character sections.

continue steering; ongoing regression correction scope, not production arithmetic.

with progress/cancel/timing. Exact BugGrabber warning in runstate/evidence, causal

#### Design / serial queue (READY)

- [x] **36. Cooperative test execution and safe checkpoints.** _Pure coroutine runner/Run compatibility and safe internal variant/budget checkpoints delivered; original96 assertion tokens retained with checkpoint plumbing only. Lua execution NOT RUN; independent static verification pending._ Add a pure in-memory
  `SelfTests.NewRunner(cases?)` with Step/Cancel and observable counts/lines/status.
  Optional cases list is isolated test injection; default uses a fixed shallow copy
  of registered cases in original order. Use native Lua5.1 coroutines: each case
  coroutine calls `t.fn(checkpoint)`, ordinary old functions may ignore that argument.
  Resume ONE checkpoint-sized unit per Step, count PASS/FAIL only after the entire
  case finishes/errors, one result line per case. `checkpoint(label?)` yields only
  at a safe unit boundary; labels bounded<=128bytes/control/markup clean. NewRunner
  max512cases/10000steps, malformed inputs/unexpected yield/error fail/cancel explicitly,
  never infinite scheduler or truncated successful suite. Cancellation releases the
  coroutine/large synthetic fixture references and is INCOMPLETE, not all-tests pass;
  no later Step executes cancelled work. Capture case/index/unit identity for timing.
  Keep `SelfTests.Run()` synchronous for approved standalone and test callers, draining
  the same runner/checkpoints and returning passed/failed/lines compatibility plus
  truthful completion fields. No frame/timer/live API/real DB access inside pure runner.

  Add checkpoints **inside expensive cases/helpers**, not just between96 whole cases:
  every large-budget Copy/Open/Apply/ViewModel/Archive/Delete operation separately,
  between native35-path parameter variants and repeated captures, large formatting
  variants and bounds matrices. Helper functions can accept/pass optional checkpoint
  callbacks; no coroutine yield inside a protected `pcall`/C API/production operation.
  In particular never yield in trappedCapture while C.Apply/K.Evaluate temporarily
  patched. At checkpoint, verify those namespace function identities match case-entry
  baseline before yielding; unsafe attempted checkpoint throws and lets existing
  helper cleanup run. Assert synthetic overrides restored on case return/yield too;
  unexpected drift stops INCOMPLETE and asks reload, never silently overwrites unknown
  function changes. All real production modules remain frozen. Any new runner fixture
  that needs callbacks uses only local fake cases, not recursion into the whole suite.

  Preserve all existing96 names/order/assertions/parameter combinations, stress exact
  50000/50001 boundaries and alias invariants. Checkpoint plumbing/function signatures
  may change, but no skipped tests, reduced synthetic sizes, no-op mocks of real copier
  work or alternate math predicate. No yielding halfway through assertions that rely
  on a temporary shared namespace mutation. Individual bounded Core operations cannot
  be preempted; don't claim a hard per-frame CPU bound until native measurements.

### K3b — approved resumable read-only knowledge capture and formatting

**Goal/why/authorization.** User Yes (2026-11-06) to broader refactor after K3a user
INCOMPLETE62/1110FAIL, case63 maximum-report combinedunit140.4ms/wall20823.1ms. R5
passed. This authorizes editing KnowledgeCheck in addition to fixtures/docs, NOT
production accounting/scheduler/threshold changes. Source limits/report semantics and
all111 old assertions preserved; no current-version rerun/performance PASS assumption.

#### Design / serial queue (implemented; review/native gates pending)

- [x] **40. Bounded KnowledgeCheck jobs and compatible synchronous APIs.** Refactor
  existing capture/formatter algorithm in place, retaining helpers/candidate/cost/
  privacy/source-pin/text-schema meaning. New APIs:
  `NewCaptureJob(env,runtime,options?)` and `NewFormatJob(report,options?)`, job
  `Step()/Cancel(reason?)`, observable status(running/complete/incomplete), completed,
  steps, phase and bounded label. Optional options.stepLimit only LOWERS default10000
  (integer1..10000); invalid options/caps fail closed, never raise collection/report
  limits. Capture and formatting data-completeness is distinct from job completion:
  a fully executed capture with missing APIs/output clipping can be job complete while
  report.captureComplete=false. In-progress/cancelled/cap/error jobs never expose a
  successful report/text/summary; release coroutine/work/env/runtime/input/buffer refs
  on terminal state except permitted sanitized terminal results. No timer/frame/DB/
  UI/copy/network/gameplay APIs in jobs, no import of SelfTests or K.Evaluate.

  Use native Lua5.1 coroutine or explicit phased state machine; resume/execute at
  most ONE fixed work unit per Step. Per-call API pcall remains. NEVER yield through
  whole capture pcall (current413–415) or other protected C/Lua operation; coroutine
  resume catches unexpected graph failures, or stage-protect before returning. Retain
  sanitized failure reason/payload omission and no candidates on failed capture.
  Capture owns private report until terminal, includes a metadata/boundary unit,
  per-tab currency/root units, at most ONE complete path collection per unit, bounded
  reconciliation work split per tab, then separate formatter units. Existing8tabs/
  64paths pertab/16children/32entries/64currencies/16costs remain exact and explicit.
  Include all paths even if eventual text output clips; don't stop traversal early
  to reduce stress. Context/config/source/runtime revision+serial/staging boundaries
  span the WHOLE collection interval, not each slice independently. Any mid-job
  changes fail stable/aggregates per existing rules; no resetting start tokens or
  freshening. Capture times end at collection finish; pure formatting does not query
  current context or claim capture-time data is still live at render time.

  Formatter uses same deterministic encode/body/trailer logic,256KiB/4096lines with
 4096byte reserve. A format unit processes at most EIGHT add/line attempts (including
  no-op clipped attempts) or a bounded phase/footer transition. Encode of one already
  bounded record may be atomic; no preemption claim. Final concat<=256KiB atomic. No
  skipped content before clipping, changed field order/labels, new serialized job keys,
  modified clipped-success guards or borrowed candidates. Preserve all limits even
  when job spans frames; finished output same bytes for stable unchanged input.
  Input to public NewFormatJob is caller-owned immutable/borrowed until completion;
  document this and never mutate it. Capture uses its private owned report. No costly
  global Core.Copy of full512report (which can exceed snapshot budget) as a format
  snapshot workaround. Do not expose partial text as a complete report.

  Public `Capture(env,runtime)` remains SYNCHRONOUS, internally drains NewCaptureJob
  to terminal and returns same-shaped sanitized report. Public `Format(report)`
  remains synchronous, drains NewFormatJob and returns same text/effective summary.
  Existing /ca knowledgecheck command/Show behavior unchanged, NOT newly asynchronous
  or a performance guarantee for arbitrary real manual capture. Finish logic applies
  existing outputTruncated clearing of candidate/shared/trees even after collection
  complete; captures which unexpectedly fail may be terminal INCOMPLETE but still
  return sanitized incomplete evidence through compatibility wrapper, never raw error.
  Unexpected format/cap failure must yield bounded unmistakably incomplete version1
  fallback, no partial-success summary; cancel releases results/references and wrapper
  must not mislabel cancellation as complete. Job errors isolated, no global monkeypatch
  or throwing beyond wrappers. Stable normal/error-API fixture semantics unchanged.

- [x] **41. Slice full stress work and add job regressions.** _(depends40)_ Existing
  knowledgeFixture(shape,checkpoint?) may accept an optional safe callback and pause
  after complete synthetic per-path/tab setup, with no change for callers withoutarg.
  Case63 alone switches maximum512 capture and BOTH subsequent formatting operations
  to job Step loops, pausing in OUTER test after each returned Step (not inside job

  Append isolated regressions: stepped vs synchronous report/data/text parity on
  fresh equivalent synthetic normal/zero/error/mismatch/clipped inputs; no partial
  report/text/summary before terminal; onepath/8lineunit accounting; independent jobs/
  repeated Step/Cancel and reference release; cancellation before capture/between
  paths/during formatting; lower step cap/invalid options bounds; unexpected private
  errors with no payloads; context/source/config/revision/serial/staging changes BETWEEN
  slices invalidating whole capture; protected API failures/statuses/empty vs nil still
  captured; pure immutable formatting, budget/footer behavior and no clipped candidates.
  Use checkpoints between sizable synchronous compatibility calls and between job
  Steps so new fixtures don't reintroduce grouped long work. No recursion/draining

**Round1 — reported native agreement/responsiveness/no normal-use errors; evidence below:**
1. Use current reviewed build, out of combat, open your own ready Midnight Inscription
   normally with no pending specialization changes. No crafting/spending/unlocking.
2. Run `/ca scan`, then `/ca`. Observe whether the game/dashboard stays responsive;
   report any load/DB-disabled/error or execution warning. Stop on those issues.
3. Report all four dashboard tree labels and points/capacities, separate Unspent KP,
   and any unknown/stale explanation. Compare to native UI wherever it provides a
   genuinely comparable value. If it doesn't show a per-tree total, say so; do not

   overflow or disagreement. Numbers alone aren't proof of future/all-rank costs.

   SavedVariables upload is required for this first round.

**Round2 — values/time preservation reported; original check:** close the profession window and run `/ca`
to view known data. An optional explicit `/ca scan` while closed must be blocked
without clearing the previous snapshot. Check stale/last-observed versus fresh labels.

**Round3 — persistence reported; original check:** `/reload` with profession closed,
then `/ca` before reopening it. Verify prior values/times persist as last-observed,
not freshly recollected. No known value should silently become zero or disappear.
Normal opening/second scan can then verify refresh/hover if useful, not a stress loop.

### U01 — concise player tooltips / Shift-hover diagnostics

#### Goal

Remove the debug-heavy normal-hover experience without hiding useful player data or
discarding diagnostic evidence. User approval2026-11-07 “Yes, lets do that.”

#### Why (pointer)

PLAN D07/U01 and append-only Q05 follow-up; V01 usability feedback. V01's native
comparison/preservation/reload observations remain evidence, not fullT02acceptance.

#### Run state

PARTIAL:55→57 local implementation/regressions/docs/checks complete; primary exact
comparison and independent static review PASS/no blockers. No executable remainder or
design blocker. Next user scoped three-file update and focused hover observation;
native usability remains pending, not a requirement to rerun fullclient selftests.
Current workspace pre-edit snapshot retained at `/tmp/opencode/craftarmy-u01-writer-preedit`.
164/164 addon+744/565 supplements+23/23 diagnostics+both Pythonchecks PASS; native NOT RUN.
Remain PARTIAL pending focused user hover check; review evidence below is current.

#### Design — numbered pieces

- [x] **55. Add separate concise view-model summaries. Local implementation/tests complete.**
  Keep existing `V.Field` state flags/text, `.tooltip` diagnostic arrays on row/gear/
  trees and existing treeDetail generation intact; add `summaryTooltip` on gear/trees
  and `cellTooltips` indexed by V.index on rows. Separate summary-building helpers
  may read already available values/states, never mutate snapshots/session/diagnostics.
  Capture field states in Row's existing field helper rather than new collection.
  Main table cells/ratios/sorting/warnings/known counts/lifecycle unchanged.

  Default cell content:
  Name/Realm/Profession: full identity/profession labels (no record dump).
  Skill: observed base level/max and observed modifier if known, own freshness.
  Concentration/Moxie/Unspent: own quantity/cap where observed, own freshness, applicable
  warning only. No projection/cap invention. Unspent0 remains known, notunknown.
  LastUpdated: last scan relative age, saved/current/archived/stale context and reminder
  fields may have independent observation times; exact timestamps available in Shift
  diagnostics. Do not use archivedAt or attemptedAt in place of scannedAt/observedAt.
  Knowledge cell: short tree overview/unspent separate; bar: tree name, purchased/
  capacity if known, own freshness, at most6 branch lines in original traversal order,
  overflow count +Shift hint, concise caveat `Future point costs are unverified.` for
  native-rank-reconciled data. Branch counts are individual path points (not subtree
  totals). Root `Main branch`, descendants deterministic ordinal labels (e.g.Branch1,
  Branch1.1); no invented observed/localized names or bare IDs. Known locks shown,
  unknown availability not called unlocked/spend-ready. Locked capacities retained.
  Unknown/stale totals don't turn raw data into numbers; old tree totals continue
  last-observed/stale when present. Missing paths explained without raw ID dump.

  Gear summary: role, empty/not-applicable vs unknown/loading, independently known
  crafted quality/rarity; preserve observed stats/enchants player information on tool.
  Stat summary: full mapped names+amounts, no raw stat keys; unmapped uses honest
  `Unknown stat` label+observed amount. One short attribution caveat (observed totals,
  base/enchant split unknown; no arithmetic). Enchant summary: known category, confirmed
  no enchant vs unknown vs no tool/notapplicable; never infer enchant amount/rank.
  Keep meaningful observed enchant text (up to2 nonempty lines; overflow Shift hint).
  Raw enchantID/slotID/sourcePin/policy/generation/status tokens only diagnostics.
  Native item tooltip remains only A1/A2/Tool, not Stat/Enchant.

  Summary state wording uses existing semantics: fresh guarded observation => current
  observation, no active-session freshness => last observed snapshot, failed latest
  attempt with retained last => stale/refresh unavailable/failed, never zero/empty.
  No snapshot => not observed; known empty is distinct. Use observedAt for field age;
  invalid/missing time unknown, not now. Archived summaries explicitly archived and
  never fresh/active warning. Raw reasons in diagnostics, meaningful summary on default.
  Keep summary <=16 addon lines per hover (excluding native/thirdparty item text),
  with explanatory overflow where needed; no destructive truncation of diagnostic
  arrays or snapshot strings. Full identities retained on identity hover.

- [x] **56. Route default vs Shift-hover in Dashboard. Local implementation/tests complete.**
  Extend existing local tooltip routing, no events/polling/settings/new window.
  Normal hover uses new focused summaries, final `Shift-hover for diagnostics.` hint
  where diagnostics exist. Holding Shift BEFORE entering hover selects existing full
  diagnostic arrays. Use env.IsShiftKeyDown only when callable, pcall and accept true;
  missing/throwing/nonboolean => concise default, not debug fallback or error.
  Default status hover only Dashboard.StatusText; Shift adds original raw status/block
  detail. Unknown/blocking reason remains visible (don't hide actual failure behind
  generic success). Header/lifecycle/confirmation behavior unchanged. Default field
  cells never reuse row-wide dumps. Shift for nongear cells can retain original
  identity+row diagnostics; Shift for tool/stat/enchant retains original tool evidence.
  Preserve ClearLines/SetOwner/SetHyperlink/AddLine order, no native/thirdparty stripping;
  PlainTooltip still sanitizes only addon-supplied text. Hide on leave, no stale links
  across gear→Stat/Enchant/name/header transitions, no accumulating diagnostic arrays.

- [x] **57. Regression fixtures/docs/local checks. Local complete; review/native hover pending.**
  Preserve all149 existing assertions/workloads/stress sizes/checkpoints/timing limits.
  ONLY allowable existing case changes: add a fixture-local IsShiftKeyDown=true in
  the R5 raw-status case, R5 native-item/full-evidence case and R6 Enchant evidence
  case, so their original technical-hover assertions target the opt-in path unchanged.
  No other existing bodies/helpers/runner change. Append new U01 cases (in a `do`
  block to avoid Lua local limits) before Tests.Run; reuse existing fixture helpers
  within their scope or create bounded test-local inputs. Extra new cases legitimate;
  report actual resulting casecount, historical149 remains historical. K6/K7 entire
  standalone runner (744/565) byte-frozen. No tests disabled/skipped/manufacturedPASS.

  New tests MUST exercise default AND Shift real UI OnEnter paths for status/identity/
  Skill/Concentration/Moxie/Unspent/LastUpdated/tool/stat/enchant/tree, native hyperlink
  only gear, ClearLines+owner order/clean stale links/hide, no native/thirdparty stripping,
  Shift absent/false/error/nonboolean fallback, no mutation/accumulation across repeated
  hover/rebuild. Assert default absence of raw IDs/keys/source pins/generation/raw
  status, preserved diagnostic content, field-specific counts/freshness and warnings,
  unknown vs observed0 vs confirmedempty; fresh/stale/no-session/archived ages,
  stale-tree last-value vs current-invalid-raw, totals/caveat/locked branch capacity,
  >6 branch overflow and <=16lines, unknown/unmapped enchant, observed amounts and
  attribution caveat, exact timestamps remain in diagnostics. Existing149 unchanged
  assertions still run. DB/session/source data compare before/after.

  Inspect then run installed `lua5.1 tests/dashboard.lua` (all addon cases+744+565),
  `lua5.1 tests/run.lua`23/23, both `python3 tools/check_dashboard.py` and
  `python3 tools/check_diagnostics.py`. Missing native clock correctly unavailable;
  local tests aren't WoW validation. No install/shim/globalmath/clock/profiler changes.
  README top current U01 behavior+review-gated update ONLYViewModel/Dashboard/SelfTests
  (SelfTests newregressions only, no fullclient rerun), Shift-before-hover workflow;
  after primary review focused user hover/unknown/stale usability check, not V01 loop

  counts/freeze. Stop PARTIAL pending review/nativehover, don't claim shipped/fixedinWoW.

#### Design — numbered pieces

- [x] **58. Optional observed localized path names. Local complete; review/native pending.**
  Collector knEntry already reads GetEntryInfo(configID,entryID). Project optional
  `definitionID` when valid using existing knInt guard; do not require it or alter
  metadataComplete. After each path's existing node/unlock/spend reads, select entry
  matching node.activeEntry.entryID against unlockEntryID/spendEntryID. If no match or
  definitionID unavailable, name absent; no new GetEntryInfo calls or arbitrary choice.
  Read C_Traits.GetDefinitionInfo(definitionID), guarded knRead/plain table/rawget;
  prefer nonempty valid overrideName, else if valid spellID read guarded
  C_Spell.GetSpellName(spellID). At most one definition and one spell-name read per
  captured path (existing64paths/tree8trees). No queues/timers/requests/events/cache.
  Normalize controls/pipes to spaces, trim; only accept1..256bytes and nonblank.
  Do not truncate a long/localized name midcodepoint; invalid/too-long => absent.
  Persist OPTIONAL `p.name`; IDs stay separate, never use name for traversal/accounting.
  Name API missing/nil/error/malformed does not change numeric/nativeComplete/coverage/
  staged status, point totals or other fields. No raw error payload persisted.

  Core path validator adds ONLY optional p.name check: nonblank string<=256bytes,
  no control/pipe; all other Core logic BYTEFROZEN. Existing entry.definitionID
  validation already exists; no schema/TOC/version bump/migration/reset. Knowledge.lua
  remains BYTEFROZEN (already copies full raw path). Old snapshots with no name valid;
  optional names survive Copy/Evaluate/Apply/Open/reload/archive by current pipeline.
  Failed numeric scan keeps previous coherent named tree; never borrow current-raw
  labels onto retained old tree. Label-only failure may leave unknown label on new
  otherwise-valid snapshot; no fabricated fallback/history cache needed.

## Tests (add; keep the suite green)

Approved local commands; inspect check scripts before running:

```text
python3 tools/check_diagnostics.py
python3 tools/check_dashboard.py
lua5.1 tests/run.lua          # only if already installed
lua5.1 tests/dashboard.lua    # only if already installed
```

In-client equivalent: `/cad selftest` and `/ca selftest`, **user-run** after delivery.
No package installation/download or substitute runtime is authorized. Structural
checks verify files/TOC order/path containment/metadata, one production DB name and
no addon dependencies; they do not parse Lua or prove API behavior. Docs checks must
verify local links and unchanged diagnostic source hashes against retained evidence.

Required new pure regressions (table-driven subcases are acceptable):

1. Zero/false vs nil/error; transient preservation with distinct attempt/observation
   times; malformed/future schema fails without overwriting DB; cross-GUID isolation.
2. Valid empty vs unloaded gear; independent uncommon/rare/epic and crafted ranks
   1–5; unknown rank; unknown role; swapped-item metadata invalidation; late callback,
   timeout, request bounds, profession-switch/closed/linked/guild/NPC/combat contexts.
3. Enchant absent/present/unmapped/conflicting/unready; 8005 category without invented
   amount/rank; multiple stats and ambiguous base/enchant attribution, no double count.
4. Knowledge mixed unlock sizes **0 and 1**, locked zero path, all subbranches,
   zero/max investment, missing entry and invalid ranks, increased/free ranks,
   unknown/variable costs, staged/unknown staging, cycle/duplicate/shared path,
   late root and four tabs (35-path shape), limits/missing child data. Never
   publish incomplete denominators; keep last coherent tree on failure.
5. Shared spent counted once; reconciliation mismatch; zero/missing unspent;
   one tree cannot borrow another's ranks/cap or overwrite a last-valid aggregate.
6. Moxie **599/600/601**; concentration **899/900/1000** of verified 1000, unknown/zero
   caps; historical vs fresh warning labels; unknown missing-enchant/progression state;
   sorting/filter ties and archived exclusion; no offline concentration projection.
   Assert accessory #1 → accessory #2 → tool display order, with tool stats and
   enchant status/category grouped under Tool and distinct from each other.
7. Complete vs partial roster removal, retirement, immutable archive, relearn fresh
   generation, scoped confirmation/cancel/delete; old generation cannot leak into new.
8. Injected API spies: only current player reads and own DB writes; self-tests never
   touch real DB; commands/rendering do not invoke crafting/spending/equipment changes,
   Auctionator, inventory, diagnostic mutation, or unbounded timers/scans.

**Primary/user runtime acceptance checklist (not assigned agent actions):**

- Safe manual install and `/ca selftest`; actual load/command/UI errors recorded.
  Existing `/cad selftest` still passes separately; old 23/23 does not prove T02.
- Open own Midnight Inscription: compare skill/modifier, live currencies, three
  rarity/rank indicators, actual tool stats and enchanted 8005 category to native UI.
  Historical balances/items are not expected current values. Do not ask user to buy,
  enchant, equip, unlearn or spend knowledge just to produce a test fixture.
- Capture each of the four knowledge trees' **actual unlock-entry maxRanks**, complete
  path coverage and committed ranks; compare tree/path totals and capacities to native
  UI and shared spent once. Record discrepancies. Conditional T01 caps are not truth.
  Unknown arithmetic is safe degradation, **not a passed knowledge-bar feature**.
- Reload/relog preserves DB; second character/profession remains separate; offline
  row has timestamped last-observed concentration and no invented regeneration.
  Closed/linked/unready/unsafe contexts and delayed item data preserve valid snapshots.
- Check scrolling, filters/sorts, tooltips and current-vs-historical passive warnings.
  Boundary values/archive/relearn may use fixtures if unavailable safely in client;
  fixture-only lifecycle evidence must be labeled, not claimed as live coverage.
- Missing optional addons do not matter. Other professions are tested as available;
  unknown mappings remain visibly unknown and overall eight-profession readiness stays
  INCOMPLETE until separately evidenced. No Auctionator/bank test required for T02.

Acceptance gates are separate: **delivery → independent review → primary/user client
acceptance → any separately approved release**. Inscription acceptance requires its
required fields/knowledge semantics verified or explicitly CHANGES REQUIRED; a safe
`?` prevents false data but does not complete a requested field. Full eight-profession
dashboard and guides remain future work. Do not close Q03/Q04 wholesale with T02.

## Out of scope (do NOT do)

Guides/routes/knowledge-source tracking, shopping/inventory/banks, Auctionator calls
or lists, concentration estimates, automated purchases/crafts/equipping/enchants/
knowledge spending, cross-account sync, broad localization/distribution work,
modifying diagnostics or historical evidence, dependency installations, game installs,
publication/deployment/Git mutations. No broad rewrites or new architecture choices
by the implementer; unresolved facts get bounded unknowns and an evidence gap.

## Report back

Persist T02 state and `docs/dashboard/evidence.md`; then return status, completed/
blocked pieces and dependents, changed files and hashes, exact check outputs or
NOT RUN reasons, known safety/semantic limits and the next primary/user action.
Do not put the only recovery information in chat. If a required API fact prevents
exact accounting, return the precise missing inputs/source rather than guessing.
